# SCORECARD (AFTER) — NBD Pro CRM audit, re-swept 2026-11-07

**Read this first.** This file is the same generator ([build-scorecard.js](../../scripts/audit/build-scorecard.js)) run over the 2026-11-07 ~01:20 ET browser sweep. Only the **browser columns** (1 Reach, 4 Mobile, 6 A11y, tenant-B leak) were re-measured. The code-read cells come from the slice files in [inventory/](inventory/), which were not re-scored after the fixes, so they still show the 2026-11-06 audit. For what was fixed, use [findings-status](findings-status.md) and [MORNING_REPORT](MORNING_REPORT.md). The before version is [SCORECARD](SCORECARD.md).

The after sweep ran on the steward session's emulator, serving commit 8f250947 (main minus 18 commits), with the same seed, routes and three users. It was read-only.
